- [x] Add notification center upgrade: real-time delivery, unread grouping, task reminders, approval requests, export completion, failed worker jobs, campaign warnings, scoring drift alerts, and one-click deep links. **Real-time delivery, task reminders, and now one-click deep links are built; unread grouping, and several event categories, are not.** Real-time delivery (Postgres LISTEN/NOTIFY over SSE) and a working bell/dropdown already existed. Added this pass: every notification is now clickable — `resolveNotificationLink` reads the same `data` payload (`entityType`/`entityId`, `leadId`/`opportunityId`, `viewId`, `taskId`) every existing `createUserNotification` call site already writes, and routes to the real Lead/Opportunity detail page or the View deep-link pattern established earlier this session (falling back to a list page for Activities/Tasks/Partners, which have no detail route); clicking now marks that one notification read via a new `markAsRead` (calling the existing single-notification-capable `PATCH /api/notifications`) instead of requiring "Clear all." **"Unread grouping" is not built, and isn't meaningfully applicable to the current architecture without a larger redesign**: the SSE snapshot only ever fetches `"isRead" = false` rows, so the client-side notification list is, by construction, always 100% unread — there is no "read but still shown" state to group against. **Event coverage remains narrow**: approval requests, export completion, failed worker jobs, campaign warnings, and scoring drift alerts have zero `createUserNotification` call sites anywhere in the codebase (only task SLA/reminder/escalation, lead/opportunity reassignment, and saved-view comment/update/access-request exist) — adding those is a larger, separate effort per event source.

Fixed with a real `CAMPAIGN_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS` guard (mirroring `payouts.ts`'s own `ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS` map), same-status re-invocation still allowed as a no-op (needed by `launchMarketingCampaignForTenant`'s own existing re-entrant call). **Partner changes, built from scratch -- the one domain with genuinely no approval concept at all**: confirmed by audit that `PATCH /api/partners/[id]` was already tenant-admin-only, so a partner had never had ANY way, self-service or otherwise, to change their own profile. New `PartnerChangeRequest` table (migration `0097`) plus `src/lib/server/partner-change-requests.ts` (`submitPartnerChangeRequest`/`approvePartnerChangeRequest`/`rejectPartnerChangeRequest`, atomic claim, real comments via `reviewComment`) -- a partner proposes changes to their legal/financial identity fields only (`legalBusinessName`/`gstin`/`panNumber`/`registeredAddress`/`registeredState`, not operational fields like `canAccessPayouts`), which only actually apply once approved, reusing the exact existing `updatePartnerProfileForTenant` write path rather than a second one. - [x] Add recent/favorite records: recent leads/opportunities/tasks/views/reports/campaigns, pin/favorite support, keyboard navigation, and quick-open from command palette. **All 6 record types now have real recent-view tracking AND favorite/pin toggles, extending the earlier, deliberately narrow Lead/Opportunity-only "jump to recent records" sub-item this checklist's own prior note referenced.** `src/lib/recent-records.ts` generalized from a 2-type union to the full `"lead" | "opportunity" | "task" | "view" | "report" | "campaign"`, and gained a genuinely new, separate `FavoriteRecord` concept (`getFavoriteRecords`/`isFavoriteRecord`/`toggleFavoriteRecord`) alongside the pre-existing capped-at-8 `RecentRecord` list -- favorites are deliberately uncapped and only ever change via an explicit toggle, never automatically. The Campaign list required a real fix, not a workaround: its row was a single `<button>` wrapping the whole card, and nesting an interactive Star button inside it would have been invalid HTML -- fixed by converting the row to a `div[role="button"]` with an `onKeyDown` handler for Enter/Space (preserving keyboard operability) so the nested Star button can call `stopPropagation()` correctly, rather than leaving the toggle unbuilt for this one type. **Quick-open deep links**, following this app's established `window.location.search` convention (no `useSearchParams`, since none of these pages are wrapped in Suspense): Tasks' pre-existing `?taskId=` support reused, Views' pre-existing `?viewId=` support reused, Marketing gained a new `?campaignId=`, and Reports gained a new `?reportId=` -- the last one required routing around a real architectural wrinkle: `CustomReportsSection` (the saved-reports list) and `CustomReportBuilder` (the tab that actually displays a loaded report) are separate components that only communicate via a `custom-report-edit` window event, and `CustomReportsSection` only mounts when the "Saved Reports" tab is active, so a deep link landing on the default "Overview" tab would silently do nothing. Fixed by having `CustomReportBuilder` itself fetch `/reports/custom` and dispatch the load event on mount, and having `ReportsPage`'s existing `create=1`-tab-switch effect also switch to the Builder tab on `?reportId=`. **Command palette** (`global-search.tsx`): `recentIcon`/`recentPath` (lead/opportunity-only) replaced with `recordTypeIcon`/`recordTypePath` covering all 6 types with the correct per-type deep-link route, and a new, separate "Favorites" `Command.Group` (uncapped, sourced from `getFavoriteRecords()`) added alongside the existing capped "Recent" group. **Keyboard navigation confirmed already satisfied for free**: `cmdk`'s `Command.Item`s across Favorites/Recent/Commands/search-result groups share one list, so native arrow-key navigation works across all of them with no new code required. 15 new/updated tests in `tests/recent-records.test.ts` (SSR-safety for all favorite functions, add/remove/re-toggle, the uncapped-vs-capped-at-8 distinction, malformed-input rejection, and independence between the recent and favorite stores for the same record). `tsc`, full `eslint` on every touched file, full `vitest` suite (1500/1500, no regressions), and `npm run build` all verified clean; no live authenticated click-through (no test credentials in this environment).
